# Plan: Fork Handy into Mouthy (Phase 1 — app shell + HTTP backend; Phase 2 — offline Kokoro)

This is the project's living engineering log, kept as the plan of record while the app was built. "Human Asks" are decisions the maintainer made.

Created: 2026-10-12 · Status: confirmed 2026-10-12 (H1 yes, H8 keep handy-keys) · Phase 2 signed off 2026-10-14 (H12) · Complexity: high · Confidence: 82%
Design lane: not applicable · Intent-critical: yes

The default output was the MacBook Pro speakers at P2.3. App log times are UTC. Kokoro-FastAPI at 127.0.0.1:8880 hung during T2, T3b, T4 and T5 and was slow at P2.3 (1.6–8.2 s for a first sentence); agents never restart or signal it. The ORT archive is cached in `~/Library/Caches/ort.pyke.io`.
- **Debug launch hands off:** while the user's release Mouthy.app runs, a debug launch connects through single-instance (`/tmp/com_azizi_mouthy_si.sock`, hard-coded), raises the release app's Settings window and exits at once. The tauri-specta export runs before single-instance, so `bindings.ts` regeneration still works; live checks do not.
- **Dev app-data:** `models/kokoro-v1.0/` holds `int8.onnx` and the voices `af_alloy`, `bf_emma`, `bm_lewis`.
- **Dev settings store:** sha256 `fb27d456…`, `tts_backend: local`, `keyboard_implementation: tauri`, bindings `{speak: option+escape}`; the first launch of a post-P3.2 build adds `speak_clipboard`. Stages that change it for a live run restore it byte for byte.
- **Bindings export:** `cd src-tauri && perl -e 'alarm 20; exec @ARGV' ./target/debug/mouthy --start-hidden` (exit 142 expected; a full hidden launch with a tray icon for 20 s).
- **Active constraints:** Phase 2 approved deps only: `ort` (`=2.0.0-rc.14`, upgrade to 2.0 final when released), `misaki-rs` (default-features off), `sha2`, `futures-util`; anything else needs asking; one commit per logical change; every stage passes the per-stage check list; macOS first; delete, don't disable; never commit model files.
- **Invariants for any further change:** every speak-session change runs in an `actions::*_on_main` fn on the main thread, the only thread that locks `TtsState`; those fns never call the posting entry points (`speak_selection`, `speak_clipboard`, `speak_file`, `preview_voice`, `stop_speaking`, `request_speech`, `hotkey_released`). On Linux speak requests, hotkey releases and handed-over files go through `actions::send_in_order`, the one request thread, so `wl-paste` never runs on the main thread and presses, releases and files reach it in the order made; `stop_speaking`, `preview_voice` and the launch file post at once, and a request made before a stop that reaches the main thread after it is dropped (`STOPS`). A hotkey press stops speech only after `Session::note_release` counted a release, or at once for a voice preview or a file (`Session::without_hotkey` starts with `released_since_start` set, since no hotkey started it). `speech-state` is emitted only on the main thread where `TtsState` changes, so a replaced session's `ended` precedes its successor's `started`. Never call tray/overlay code from a thread holding a lock the main thread may take (T2 deadlock); `hide_overlay` is main-thread only. `block_on` only on the synth thread. `cargo build` has zero compiler warnings (only `build.rs`'s tray-translation notice). Release builds that link ONNX Runtime depend on `build.rs` passing `-fapple-link-rtlib`; Kokoro sessions use `AutoDevicePolicy::PreferCPU`. A local chunk is one inference of up to `tts::local::PIECE_TOKENS` (510) ids, so its intonation spans the sentence; `Backend::max_chunk_chars` cuts sentences at 450 characters for Local (`tts::local::MAX_CHUNK_CHARS`) and 300 for HTTP. ORT keeps the memory of its longest run (~1 GB at 510 ids); sessions keep its memory pattern off (`build_session`), or the arena grows ~40% past that. Each local piece is trimmed of Kokoro's silence padding (`tts::local::trim_silence`; a sentence's last piece and a piece cut at a pause keep `PAUSE_END_MS`, others `PHRASE_END_MS`, both ÷ speed; a `split_long` cut is not a sentence end, carried by `text::Chunk::ends_sentence`); the HTTP backend's audio is untouched. The overlay panel must keep `overlay::prevent_activation`, or a click on it activates Mouthy and raises Settings. The first-clause split, number expansion and `text::spell_out` (initialisms, camel case, dotted names) run for the Local backend only; the HTTP path speaks `normalize` + `split_sentences` output as Phase 1 did. One ORT `Environment` per process (`tts::local::ENVIRONMENT`, a `OnceLock`, never dropped). The loaded model (`tts::local::LOADED`) is locked only by the synth and preload threads, never the main thread; a session holds it until its synth thread ends. A preload unloads it when the selected files are missing, records its error in `tts::local::PRELOAD_ERROR` (a separate lock the async `list_model_files` reads) and then sends `model-files-changed`. Model downloads are tasks on the async runtime, cancelled through a `futures_util` `AbortHandle`; `commands::models::ModelDownloads` is locked briefly by async commands and those tasks, never across an await. Overlay payload is `OverlayView` JSON; window label `overlay`, fixed 360×60.
- **Open questions:** H4, H9, H10, H11, H22 (Human Asks). The user listened during the DoD run (2026-10-14); the user confirmed P2.8's whole sentences on 2026-10-14. Known limits: first audio is ~200–260 ms only when the first clause is ≤ 6 words; a first sentence with no clause break is one inference, ~1.3 s at 330 characters and ~1.7 s at 450 on the M4 Pro; the espeak-ng path maps symbols as hexgrad's `espeak.py` does while lexicon words keep misaki-rs's eSpeak forms (part of H10). Known limits of the T5 gate are listed in its Implementation Notes.

## Intent (verbatim)

> Mouthy is a free, open-source, offline-first desktop text-to-speech app for macOS (Windows/Linux later). Core loop: user highlights text in any app → presses a global hotkey → the text is read aloud by a local neural TTS voice → pressing the hotkey again stops playback. It is the inverse of Handy. We are forking Handy and inverting its I/O path rather than starting from scratch.
>
> Step 0 … 5. Write the module map and a phased plan to PLAN.md, and ask me to confirm it before Phase 1. Keep CLAUDE.md but rewrite it for Mouthy as the first commit after the plan is agreed.
>
> ## Hard constraints
>
> - **Rebrand completely and early.** Handy's name, logo, icon, and brand assets are NOT open source. Replace every occurrence: app name, bundle identifier (use `com.azizi.mouthy`), tauri.conf.json product name/identifier/window titles, icons (generate simple placeholder icons for now), tray tooltip, README, website links, release notes, sponsor images (delete), Discord links (delete), updater endpoint and pubkey (remove the updater entirely for now; we'll add our own later), app-data directory name. Add an attribution line in README and LICENSE noting Mouthy is derived from Handy (MIT, cjpais) and is not affiliated or endorsed.
> - **Keep Handy's architecture and conventions.** Tauri 2, Rust backend, React + TypeScript + Tailwind frontend, bun, the existing settings and command patterns, the existing lint/format config. Don't introduce new frameworks. Match the existing code style exactly.
> - **Delete, don't disable.** Remove STT code paths outright (VAD, Whisper/transcribe-cpp, Parakeet/transcribe-rs, microphone capture, dictionary/post-processing, transcript history) and their Cargo/package dependencies, so the binary and build get smaller. Keep a clean git history: one commit per logical removal.
> - **macOS first.** Everything must work on an Apple-Silicon Mac. Leave Windows/Linux code paths compiling where cheap, but don't spend effort on them.
> - **Offline by default is the end state** (Phase 2). Phase 1 may depend on a local HTTP server.
> - Ask before adding any dependency not already in the repo.
>
> ## Phase 1 — app shell with an external TTS backend
>
> Goal: Mouthy.app that fully works against a locally running OpenAI-compatible TTS server (I run Kokoro-FastAPI at http://127.0.0.1:8880, endpoint POST /v1/audio/speech, voice `bm_lewis`).
>
> Behaviour:
>
> 1. Global hotkey (default ⌥⎋ / Alt+Escape — same as macOS "Speak selection"; configurable in Settings like Handy's shortcuts). On press:
>    - If currently speaking → stop immediately.
>    - Else capture the current selection: save the clipboard, send ⌘C via the existing enigo path, poll the clipboard for a change (≤200 ms), read the text, restore the clipboard. If nothing was selected, show a brief overlay notice and do nothing.
> 2. Light text normalisation before synthesis (strip URLs → "link", collapse whitespace, drop markdown symbols, \* # ` > etc.). Keep this in one small, tested Rust function.
> 3. Split the text into sentences, synthesise sentence-by-sentence, and start playback as soon as the first sentence's audio is ready while the rest is still synthesising (bounded channel/queue). Playback via the audio-output path already present (cpal/rodio — use whatever Handy already uses for audio feedback; don't add a second audio stack).
> 4. Overlay (reuse Handy's recording overlay) shows a "speaking" state while audio plays; hides on stop/finish.
> 5. Settings UI: hotkey, backend URL, voice (free-text for now, dropdown populated from the server's /v1/audio/voices if it exists), speed (0.5–2.0), volume, "launch at login", "start hidden", overlay position. Remove all STT settings.
> 6. Tray menu: Speak selection / Stop / Settings / Quit.
> 7. CLI flags via the single-instance plugin, mirroring Handy's: `--speak-selection`, `--stop`, `--start-hidden`, `--no-tray`.
> 8. Graceful failure: if the backend is unreachable, overlay shows an error and a Settings link; never crash.
>
> Definition of done for Phase 1: fresh clone → build → launch → grant Accessibility permission → highlight text in Safari and in VS Code → ⌥⎋ reads it in Lewis's voice → ⌥⎋ stops it. `cargo test` and `bun run lint` are clean. README documents setup.
>
> ## Phase 2 — in-process Kokoro (offline)
>
> Replace the HTTP backend with Kokoro-82M running inside the Rust process via ONNX Runtime (`ort` crate), keeping the HTTP backend as a selectable fallback. Reuse Handy's model-manager pattern: Settings → Models page, download the ONNX model + voices file to the app-data `models/` dir with progress, verify checksum, select active model. Phonemisation is the hard part: evaluate (a) an existing Rust Kokoro crate (e.g. `kokoros` / `kokoro-tts`) vs (b) `ort` + our own G2P via espeak-ng bindings. Write up the trade-offs (binary size, licence, maintenance, macOS build complexity, CoreML/Metal EP availability) in PLAN.md and let me choose before implementing. Target: first audio within ~300 ms of the hotkey for a sentence on an M-series Mac.
>
> ## Phase 3 — polish (only after I say so)
>
> Voice picker with per-voice preview, voice blending, a signed release build via `tauri build`, a Homebrew cask formula in a tap, proper icon, auto-update.
>
> ## Working style
>
> - Work in small commits with conventional-commit messages. Run the app and actually test the hotkey loop yourself where you can; tell me exactly what you could not verify.
> - Don't ask me questions you can answer by reading the repo. Do ask before architectural choices not covered here.
> - Keep a running CHANGELOG.md.

### (b) Plumbing kept

`lib.rs` (setup; specta `collect_commands!` exports `../src/bindings.ts` **at debug app launch**, relative to cwd — see per-stage checks), `settings.rs` (serde struct + `get_default_settings`; unknown stored keys ignored, so no migrations), `shortcut/{mod,handler,tauri_impl}.rs` (bindings generic over `settings.bindings`; Carbon hotkeys need no Accessibility, ignore Secure Input, deliver on the main thread), `actions.rs` `ShortcutAction` trait + `ACTION_MAP`, `tray.rs`+`tray_i18n.rs` (strings generated by `build.rs` from each locale's `tray` section; a missing key renders blank), `overlay.rs` (`show_overlay_state(app, &str)` → `show-overlay` event; generation counter; `hide_recording_overlay`), `audio_toolkit/audio/device.rs` + `get_cpal_host`, `clipboard.rs` `with_enigo`/`write_text_to_clipboard`, `input.rs` (`EnigoState`, macOS layout-aware `command_v_key()` scanning keycodes 0..128 with `UCKeyTranslate` — main-thread only), `autostart.rs` (objc2 SMAppService), `cli.rs` + single-instance forwarding (`lib.rs:856`; `CliArgs::parse` runs first so every forwarded flag must be declared), `signal_handle.rs`, frontend `App.tsx`/`Sidebar.tsx`, `hooks/useSettings.ts`, `stores/settingsStore.ts` (every persisted key needs a `settingUpdaters` entry), `AccessibilityPermissions`/`AccessibilityOnboarding` (accessibility only after R2), `components/ui`, i18n (`fallbackLng: "en"`; `check:translations` exits 1 on missing/extra keys vs `en`), `tests/app.spec.ts` (keep; rename describe).

### (c) Hotkey → action path, and its inversion

Handy: key event → `handle_shortcut_event` → `TranscriptionCoordinator` (debounce, PTT/hold/toggle) → `TranscribeAction::start` (model, mic, VAD, overlay, tray) → `stop` → transcribe → post-process → `clipboard::paste` (save → write → ⌘V → delay → restore).
Mouthy (D10): Carbon hotkey on main thread → `handle_shortcut_event` → `ACTION_MAP["speak"]` → `SpeakAction::start` (release ignored): lock `TtsState`; if a session is current → set its stop flag, hide overlay, tray Idle; else set `Capturing`, `clipboard::capture_selection` inline (D12), normalize + split, create `Session{generation, stop}`, spawn synth thread + playback thread, overlay "speaking", tray Speaking. `--speak-selection`/`--stop`/SIGUSR2/SIGUSR1 and the tray items call the same two entry points via `run_on_main_thread` (fire-and-forget).

## Research Notes

- **rodio 0.23** (registry source): `Sink` is now `Player` (`append`, `stop`, `clear`, `empty`, `len`, `set_volume`, `sleep_until_end`; `append` after `stop` resumes; stop is immediate, 5 ms periodic). `Player::connect_new(&mixer)`. `SamplesBuffer::new(NonZero<u16>, NonZero<u32>, impl Into<Vec<f32>>)` — **f32 only**. `wav` feature = symphonia. Mixer resamples 24 kHz mono. `MixerDeviceSink` must live on the playback thread.
- **Kokoro-FastAPI live probe:** `GET /v1/audio/voices` → `{"voices":[{"id":"af_alloy","name":…}]}`. `POST /v1/audio/speech`: `input`, `voice`, `response_format` ∈ mp3|opus|aac|flac|wav|pcm, `speed` 0.25–4.0, `stream` (default true), `volume_multiplier`, `lang_code`. Two-sentence WAV 0.57 s (TTFB 21 ms); one sentence PCM 0.27 s. **WAV headers carry RIFF/data size 0xFFFFFFFF even with `stream:false`**; `pcm` = raw s16le mono 24 kHz, no header → D2.
- **reqwest 0.12** has `json`, `stream`; no `blocking`. `tauri::async_runtime::block_on` panics inside a runtime worker; single-instance callbacks and async commands run on workers → D10.
- **tokio** direct dep has no features; only STT code uses `tokio::` (`commands/audio.rs:255` `spawn_blocking` is the one kept call → switch to `tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking` in R3). `llm_client.rs` tests use `#[tokio::test]` → dev-dep stays until R4.
- **Bindings:** `option+escape` parses in both backends (reviewer-verified). Tauri path: Carbon hotkeys do not auto-repeat on macOS; `MOD_NOREPEAT` is Windows-only — covered by a 2 s hold test, not code.
- **tauri-plugin-global-shortcut holds its `shortcuts` mutex while calling the handler** → never register/unregister/is_registered inside `SpeakAction` (Handy spawns for this: `shortcut/mod.rs:86-90`).
- **objc2:** `objc2`+`objc2-service-management` → `autostart.rs`; `objc2-foundation`/`-app-kit` → `paste_tx/macos.rs` today, reused by D12 pasteboard capture. `tauri-nspanel` = overlay.
- **Icons:** `bunx tauri icon <1024 png> -o src-tauri/icons`; python3 + `sips` available (stdlib PNG writer, no deps). Tray icons: black-on-transparent (`icon_as_template(true)`); `tray.rs:198-213` has Dark/Light/Colored rows; `lib.rs:243-249` panics if the idle icon path is missing; `src-tauri/target/debug/resources/` masks deleted files in dev.
- **Brand surface:** README, `sponsor-images/`, `.github/`, `AboutSettings.tsx`, `BUILD.md`, `CONTRIBUTING*.md`, `flake.nix`+`nix/`+`.nix/`+`scripts/check-nix-deps.ts` (postinstall), `nsis/installer.nsi:220,600,771`, `tauri.conf.json`, 17 "Handy" strings in `en`, `portable.rs`, `HANDY_*` env vars (only `HANDY_NO_GTK_LAYER_SHELL` overlay.rs:122 and `HANDY_TEST_FLAG_*` utils.rs:187-201 survive the removals), Cargo `name`/`default-run`/lib `handy_app_lib` (`main.rs`), `bun.lock:6`, `Cargo.lock:2541`, `lib.rs:821,825` log file name, `lib.rs:948` window title, `DebugPaths.tsx:29,37,46`, `theme.ts:19` `"handy.theme"`, `icons/HandyTextLogo.tsx`/`HandyHand.tsx` (Sidebar, Onboarding, AccessibilityOnboarding), `src-tauri/icons/logo.png`, `resources/default_settings.json`, `autostart.rs:132-152` fixtures.
- **MUST-KEEP Handy refs:** `[patch.crates-io]` cjpais/tao forks (`Cargo.toml:173-187`) always; LICENSE/README/CHANGELOG attribution; if H8 declined: `handy-keys` crate, `handy_keys.rs`, `KeyboardImplementation::HandyKeys` persisted as `"handy_keys"`, `handy-keys-event`, `HandyKeysShortcutInput.tsx`, `to_handy_string()`.
- **Mutual dependency:** `managers/transcription.rs` imports `AudioRecordingManager` (1 use) + `audio_toolkit::{…}`; `managers/audio.rs` imports `transcription::StreamRouter`. R2 edits `transcription.rs` minimally; if >20 lines, merge R2+R3 and record in Amendments.
- **Clipboard:** Handy saves plain text only and its restore strips `org.nspasteboard.ConcealedType`/`TransientType` (`paste_tx/macos.rs:33-40` knows them) — a naive save/restore leaks passwords to clipboard managers → D12. VS Code copies the current line on ⌘C with an empty selection (`editor.emptySelectionClipboard`).
- **Verification traps:** under `tauri dev` Accessibility is charged to the terminal, not Mouthy; `tauri build` with targets `all` runs the DMG AppleScript (needs Finder automation); shell-driven CLI tests copy from the terminal; `open -a Mouthy --args` drops args.

## Decisions (defaults confirmed by H1; review ids in brackets)

- **D1 Toggle only.** Coordinator + `shortcut_activation`/`hold_threshold_ms` deleted; `SpeakAction` ignores release.
- **D2 PCM, not WAV** [A1]: `response_format:"pcm"`, `stream:false`, one request per sentence, const `24_000` Hz in `tts/http.rs`. Server WAV headers are placeholders; PCM needs no decoder and is faster to first byte.
- **D3 Speed via the server** (`speed`), never `Player::set_speed` (pitch-shifts). Volume via `Player::set_volume(tts_volume)`.
- **D4 Settings keys (new):** `tts_base_url` (`http://127.0.0.1:8880`), `tts_voice` (`bm_lewis`), `tts_speed` (1.0, clamp 0.5–2.0), `tts_volume` (1.0, 0–1), `onboarding_completed` (existing, now written by a command). Reuse `selected_output_device`, `overlay_position`, `overlay_style`, `start_hidden`, `autostart_enabled`, `show_tray_icon`, `theme`, `app_language`, `debug_mode`, `log_level`.
- **D5 Binding ids:** single `speak` (macOS `option+escape`, else `alt+escape`) replaces `transcribe`, `transcribe_with_post_process`, `cancel`. Stored bindings not in defaults are pruned in the settings merge loop [I-S8].
- **D6 Overlay:** three states `"speaking"` | `"nothing-selected"` | `"error"` (message + "Open Settings" → `showMainWindowCommand`). `RecordingOverlay.tsx`/`.css` rewritten from scratch (~60 lines each), CSS keyframe bars; auto-hide snapshots the overlay generation at **show** time [C-S3]. Window label renamed only if cheap, with `capabilities/default.json` in the same commit. _Rejected:_ merging the two notice states — neutral, no gain.
- **D7 CI/Nix:** delete all of `.github/workflows/`, `flake.nix`, `flake.lock`, `nix/`, `.nix/`, `scripts/check-nix-deps.ts` + `postinstall`, `check:model-languages` script entry [S-M3]. Release automation returns in Phase 3.
- **D8 (superseded by D12).**
- **D9 Stage order:** T1/T2 before R6 so the ⌘C chord and pasteboard code are adapted from live code, not resurrected. _Rejected:_ reordering — mild call, not worth churn.
- **D10 TTS design — no trait in Phase 1** [S-M1, C-M1, C-M2, A-2c]: `tts/text.rs` (`normalize`, `split_sentences`) and `tts/http.rs` (`async fn synthesize(client, base_url, voice, speed, text) -> Result<Vec<i16>, String>`, `async fn list_voices(client, base_url) -> Result<Vec<String>, String>`) are plain functions; a Phase 2 seam (sync trait/enum) is added when a second backend exists. Pipeline lives in `actions.rs` (T2; `SpeakAction` is a thin wrapper over `speak_selection`): one std **synth thread** calling `tauri::async_runtime::block_on` per sentence (checks the stop flag before each request; `block_on` nowhere else — commands and the single-instance callback only `spawn`), `std::sync::mpsc::sync_channel::<Vec<i16>>(2)`; one std **playback thread** that exclusively owns the rodio `Player`, loops on `rx.recv_timeout(20 ms)`, only `recv`s the next chunk when `player.len() < 2` (real read-ahead bound), converts `s as f32 / 32768.0` into `SamplesBuffer::new(nz!(1), nz!(24_000), Vec<f32>)`, on stop calls `player.stop()` and returns, waits for end with `while !player.empty() && !stopped { sleep 20 ms }`. `TtsState { current: Mutex<Option<Session>> }`, `Session { stop: Arc<AtomicBool> }` (amended T2b: identity is `Arc::ptr_eq` on the stop flag; every transition runs on the main thread, the only thread that locks `TtsState`; on a synth error or synth-thread panic playback waits for the queued audio before the error notice; a drop guard on the playback thread posts the session end with its own flag, so a stale guard is a no-op). Reason: shared flags and `Player::append`-after-`stop` let a stale session play over a new one; `append` never blocks so a bounded channel alone bounds nothing.
- **D11 Shortcut backend (H8, recommended Tauri-only)** [S-M2, A-2a]: handy-keys exists for fn/modifier-only/side-specific/mouse keys (b733ccc #580, a58216e #1029) and `secure_input.rs` (d001fcd #1785) for event-tap death under Secure Input; neither applies to a single ⌥⎋ toggle, and under handy-keys ⌥⎋ is dead ~3–4 s whenever a password field has focus. Carbon hotkeys deliver on the main thread, which the capture needs anyway. If declined: R-keys is skipped; T2 strips the cancel/post-process fallback from `secure_input.rs` and hands the whole capture-and-start to the main thread fire-and-forget via `run_on_main_thread` — never waits on a channel from the handy-keys manager thread (deadlocks against sync shortcut commands).
- **D12 Main-thread capture + pasteboard snapshot** [C-M3, A-3, C-S5]: Capture runs synchronously on the main thread inside `speak_selection_on_main` (amended T2b). macOS: snapshot every `NSPasteboard` item with all its types (objc2-app-kit), record `changeCount`, send ⌘C through the generalised `command_key('c')`, poll `changeCount` every 10 ms ≤ 200 ms, read the string, restore the full snapshot only if `changeCount` is unchanged since our read (late-write check). No `clear()`. Other platforms: `capture_selection` returns `Err("selection capture is macOS-only")` (compile only; amended T2b). The session is created only after capture succeeds; a press during capture queues behind it on the main thread and then stops the new speech (amended T2b). Empty-after-normalize → "nothing selected".
- **D13 Delete outright** [S-S1, S-S2, S-S3]: sound feedback/themes (keep only device selection as `audio_out::open_output`), `portable.rs` (call sites use the app-data path), settings migrations + schema version (fresh app-data under the new identifier).
- **D14 Migrations deleted in R1** (first stage that edits `settings.rs`) rather than trimmed stage by stage.
- **D15 `OverlayStyle::Live` removed in R2**; `Minimal` is default with `#[serde(alias = "live")]` for dev stores.
- **D16 Dependencies are removed in the stage that orphans them**; no sweep stage. T4 keeps a final `cargo tree` / `bun pm ls` sanity check and records binary size.
- **D17 i18n:** each removal stage deletes its subtrees in `en` and the other 25 locales in the same commit; new keys are added to `en` and seeded with the English value into the other 25 by a one-off scratchpad script (tray keys especially — `build.rs:351` renders missing as blank).
- **D18 No frontend `speak_selection` command** (it would copy Mouthy's own window); tray item + CLI only.
- **D19 Splitter:** split on `.!?` (+ trailing closing quotes/brackets), merge fragments < 20 chars forward, max-length (~300 chars) char-boundary-safe fallback split on `,;:` then whitespace. No CJK rules. `list_voices` parses only the verified `{"voices":[{id,…}]}` shape.
- **D20 Icons:** throwaway generator in the scratchpad; commit PNGs only. App icon via `bunx tauri icon`; delete `icons/{android,ios}`; tray idle + speaking black-on-transparent, `_dark` variants only if `get_icon_path` needs them; Colored rows keep compiling by pointing at the same file.
- **D21 Per-stage bindings check** [C-M5]: bindings.ts is exported at debug launch, not at `cargo build`.
- _Rejected:_ "Tauri path auto-repeats" — overstated on macOS; 2 s hold test only.

## Stages (Phase 1)

Sequential — every stage touches `Cargo.toml`, `lib.rs`, `settings.rs`, `bindings.ts`, locales. **Per-stage check list** (all must pass before commit): `cd src-tauri && cargo build && cargo test`; `cd src-tauri && ./target/debug/<bin> --list-devices` (before R3) or `perl -e 'alarm 20; exec @ARGV' ./target/debug/<bin> --start-hidden` (from R3) — **must run from `src-tauri`** — then `git diff --stat src/bindings.ts` and commit bindings with the stage; `bun run lint`; `bun run build`; `bun run format:check`; `bun run check:translations`. Each stage appends a CHANGELOG entry and uses the bracketed commit prefix.

### Stage 0 — CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md rewrite + CHANGELOG.md + prettier ignore `[docs]`

- Blocked by: H1. Files: `CLAUDE.md` (keep `Read @AGENTS.md`), `AGENTS.md` (Mouthy architecture as it will be after this plan; drop model setup, Handy GitHub-workflow rules, feature freeze), `CHANGELOG.md` (Keep-a-Changelog, Unreleased), `.prettierignore` (+ `docs/.scout-report.md`, `docs/.plan-review-round1.md`), run `bun run format` on `PLAN.md`.
- Verify: `bun run format:check` passes.
- Implementation Notes: AGENTS.md describes the post-Phase-1 app with a header line pointing at Current State for progress; shortcut section reflects H8 (handy-keys default on macOS/Windows, Tauri fallback). SIGUSR1 → stop is macOS-only because Linux leaves SIGUSR1 to WebKitGTK. `format:check` and `lint` green.

### Stage R1 — Remove transcription action path, coordinator, migrations `[refactor]`

- Blocked by: nothing. Files: Module Map row R1. `handler.rs`: look up `ACTION_MAP[binding_id]`; press → `start`, release → `stop`. `signal_handle.rs`: `send_external_input(app, binding_id)` calls the action directly. `cancel_operation`/`utils::cancel_current_operation` reduced to hide overlay + tray Idle. `get_settings` loads with serde defaults only (D14). Keep `cancel`/`test` defaults until T2.
- Interim: no hotkey does anything until T2.
- Verify: per-stage list.
- Implementation Notes:
  - **Decisions:** `actions.rs` keeps only `ShortcutAction`, `CancelAction`, `TestAction` and `ACTION_MAP` (`cancel`, `test`). `handle_shortcut_event` is a plain dispatcher (press → `start`, release → `stop`) for every binding. `signal_handle::send_external_input(app, binding_id, source)` calls `ACTION_MAP[binding_id].start` with `source` as the shortcut string (what the coordinator passed), since external triggers have no release. The binding merge in `get_settings` became `settings::sync_bindings_with_defaults` (adds missing defaults, drops stored ids not in defaults; 3 unit tests); a pruning load rewrites the store, which also drops the dead `settings_schema_version`/`shortcut_activation`/`hold_threshold_ms` keys. The dynamic cancel registration (`register/unregister_cancel_shortcut`, `CANCEL_REQUESTED/REGISTERED`, reconcile) and its Secure Input twin (`register/unregister_cancel_fallback`, `cancel_requested`, `schedule_reconcile`) went with `TranscribeAction`, their only caller; the `cancel` binding is now never registered, and `secure_input::reconcile_fallback` skips it outright so a stuck Secure Input never shadows Escape globally. Also removed because their only emitter was `TranscribeAction`: the `recording-error`/`paste-error`/`transcription-error` toasts in `App.tsx`, `RecordingErrorEvent`, and their `errors.*` locale keys; the post-processing hotkey row (`settings.postProcessing.hotkey`) and the `shortcut.bindings.transcribe*` locale entries. `utils` no longer re-exports `clipboard::*` (unused).
  - **Deviations:** `salvage_settings` is kept although Module Map row R1 lists it: it is corrupt-store recovery, not a migration — without it one invalid field (e.g. a renamed enum value during R2–T3) resets the whole store to defaults (cjpais/Handy#1619). Its tests now use the `cancel` binding. Deleted with the migrations: the frozen v0.9 store fixture test (it pinned Handy-store compatibility) and the onboarding/Chinese-script/GPU/overlay-style migrations. Left on purpose: dead `transcribe_with_post_process` skips in `tauri_impl.rs`, `handy_keys.rs`, `shortcut/mod.rs` (`resume_all_shortcuts`, `register_all_shortcuts_for_implementation`, `change_post_process_enabled_setting`) — all guarded on `post_process_enabled`, so R4's compiler errors surface them; `id == "cancel"` skips (both backends, `shortcut/mod.rs`, `secure_input.rs`) and the stale "(which is dynamic)" comment at `handy_keys.rs:430` (H8: no edits beyond compiling) go in T2 with the binding. `settings.history.transcriptionFailed` still mentions the retry icon; no history entry can be created any more and R5 deletes the page.
  - **Expected dead code (rustc `dead_code`, 114 warnings, 0 before):** left for the stage that owns each module rather than pulled forward — `input.rs` (23), `clipboard.rs` (9), `paste_tx/` (19) → T2 adapts the ⌘-key resolver/`with_enigo` (D9), R6 deletes the rest; `overlay.rs` (10, incl. `show_overlay_state`) → R2/T3; `tray.rs` `Recording`/`Transcribing` → T2; `managers/audio.rs` (10) → R2; `managers/transcription.rs` (16) → R3; `managers/history.rs` (1) → R5; `llm_client.rs` (17), `apple_intelligence.rs` (4), `AppSettings::active_post_process_provider` (1) → R4; `audio_feedback.rs` (3) → R-snd. Clippy shows nothing else new (the 10 `managers/model.rs` lints predate R1).
  - **Edge cases:** stored bindings empty → all defaults added; stored `transcribe*`/`test`/unknown ids → dropped, user's `cancel` key kept; poisoned bindings → salvage takes defaults, sync no-op; already-synced store → no write. Concurrent first reads may each write the same pruned value (idempotent); a write racing the first pruning read can be lost — same one-shot window the old merge/migration writes had. Older builds reading a pruned store re-add their default bindings and treat the missing schema version as 0, re-running migrations that are no-ops for a Mouthy store apart from clearing a picked GPU device. Release events from either backend reach `CancelAction::stop`/`TestAction::stop` (no-ops/log) instead of being filtered; `cancel` can't fire anyway.
  - **Learnings:** the export run also pruned the real dev store at `~/Library/Application Support/com.azizi.mouthy/settings_store.json` (bindings `cancel, transcribe, transcribe_with_post_process` → `cancel`; `settings_schema_version`/`shortcut_activation`/`hold_threshold_ms` gone). `HANDY_DEBUG_MIC_READY_DELAY_MS` died here with `TranscribeAction`. Tests 267 → 215 (−36 coordinator, −7 actions, −12 settings migration, +3 binding sync); 16 ignored.
  - **Grep verification:** the DoD grep is empty; so are `register_cancel`, `unregister_cancel`, `cancel_requested`, `CANCEL_REQUESTED`, `CANCEL_REGISTERED`, `send_transcription_input`, `is_transcribe_binding`, `retryHistoryEntry`, `RecordingErrorEvent`, `CURRENT_SETTINGS_SCHEMA_VERSION`, `notify_processing_finished`, `notify_cancel` across `src` and `src-tauri/src`; removed locale keys have no consumers in `src`, `src-tauri`, `tests`, `scripts`.

### Stage R-keys — SKIPPED (H8: keep handy-keys)

- Blocked by: R1, H8. Files: Module Map row R-keys; `shortcut/mod.rs` keeps only the `tauri_impl` path; `lib.rs` state/commands; `settingsStore.ts`; locales.
- Verify: per-stage list; `cargo tree | grep -c handy-keys` = 0; `bun run tauri dev` → change the `cancel` binding in Settings still registers (proves the Tauri path end-to-end).

Also orphaned and removed here: `resources/models/silero-vad-v6.2-F32.gguf` (only `managers/audio.rs` loaded it), `save_wav_file`/`verify_wav_file` (`audio_toolkit/audio/utils.rs` keeps only `read_wav_samples`, C-M4), the `windows` crate feature `Win32_Media_Audio_Endpoints` (only mute-while-recording used it), the Windows-only `should_force_show_permissions_window` in `lib.rs` (microphone-denied check), `common.loading` and `onboarding.permissions.granted` locale keys (sole users removed), `MicrophoneIcon.tsx`, and the overlay-enabled cache (`OVERLAY_ENABLED`, `update_overlay_enabled_cache`; only `emit_levels`/`emit_recording_ready` read it). `overlay.rs` has one size (256×50); `show_streaming_overlay`, the Windows streaming flag and the window-size readback are gone, and `change_overlay_style_setting` no longer repositions. `RecordingOverlay.tsx` keeps the compact pill; the waveform is a fixed travelling pulse (no levels, no arming state) and the Live/streaming branch, stream-text/phase listeners and slow-load notice are deleted with their CSS. `AccessibilityOnboarding.tsx` is Accessibility-only on macOS; every other platform completes onboarding immediately. `settingsStore` keeps output devices only (`audioDevices`/`refreshAudioDevices` removed from the store and `useSettings`).
  - **`transcription.rs` edit (A8):** 12 lines — the `AudioRecordingManager` import, the now-unused `Manager` import, and the idle-watcher "keep model loaded while recording" block. Its `audio_toolkit` imports are text/lang-ID items (R4) and stay.
  - **Deviations:** `cargo tree | grep -c rustfft` is 1, not 0 — `transcribe-rs` pulls `rustfft` transitively until R3; the direct dependency is gone. `ModelSelector.tsx` (R3) lost its `isRecording` guard on auto-select (the command is gone). The en `settings.advanced.overlay.style.description` was rewritten to drop "Live"; the other 25 locales still describe Live until T3 reworks the overlay settings copy. `onboarding.permissions.description` ("a couple of permissions") and the Accessibility description ("type transcribed text") are left for T3's copy pass.

Clippy adds only the 10 pre-existing `managers/model.rs` lints.
  - **Learnings:** tests 215 → 196 (−20 in deleted VAD/sink/clamshell/audio-manager files, +1 `legacy_live_overlay_style_deserializes_to_minimal`). `tauri-plugin-macos-permissions` (crate + npm API) stays: Accessibility checks still use it. `winreg` stays (`tray.rs`, `overlay.rs`). Windows/Linux code paths (overlay sizing, Windows-only overlay tests updated to 256×50: `(-1440, 1127, 320, 63)` and `(-1456, 1121, 352, 69)`) were edited but not compiled. `lib.rs`/`cli.rs` headless doc comments still say "no mic, no VAD" — R3 deletes that path.

Also zero hits outside locale JSON for `microphone` (except one R3 test doc comment in `engine_supervisor/supervisor.rs`), `selected_channel`, `extra_recording`, `MicrophoneMode`, `is_laptop`, `PermissionAccess`, `show_streaming_overlay`, `emit_levels`, `emit_recording_ready`, `update_overlay_enabled_cache`, `handy_recorder`, `rustfft`, `save_wav_file`, `verify_wav_file`, `CaptureSink`, `AudioVisualiser`, `refreshAudioDevices`, `audioDevices`, `OverlayStyle::Live`; `"live"` only in the serde alias and its test. `cargo tree -i handy-cpal` → no match.

### Stage R-snd — Remove sound feedback, keep output-device selection `[refactor]`

- Blocked by: R2. Files: `audio_feedback.rs` → `audio_out.rs` with `pub fn open_output(selected_device: Option<String>) -> Result<MixerDeviceSink, String>` (the `DeviceSinkBuilder` selection from `play_audio_file`), delete WAVs, `play_test_sound`, `check_custom_sounds`, `SoundTheme`, keys, UI, locale keys (D13). `tauri.conf.json` `resources` glob still valid.
- Verify: per-stage list; `ls src-tauri/resources` = icons only.
- Implementation Notes:
  - **Decisions:** `audio_out::open_output(settings_device: Option<&str>) -> Result<rodio::MixerDeviceSink, String>` (takes `&str`, so T2 passes `settings.selected_output_device.as_deref()`); same selection and logging as the deleted `play_audio_file` — `None`/`"Default"` → default device (`debug!`), named device missing → `warn!` and default. No unit test: the old selection logic had none and every path needs a real device. rodio features are now `["playback"]` only — `wav`/`flac`/`mp3`/`vorbis` dropped (no `Decoder`/`rodio::play` left; `cargo tree | grep -c symphonia` = 0; `read_wav_samples` uses `hound`). `VolumeSlider.tsx` (feedback volume) deleted too; T3 adds the `tts_volume` control. The General page's "Sound" group now holds only `OutputDeviceSelector`, no longer disabled when feedback is off. Locale keys removed in all 26: `settings.sound.audioFeedback`, `settings.sound.volume`, `settings.debug.soundTheme`; en `settings.sound.outputDevice.description` dropped "for feedback sounds" (other 25 left for T3's copy pass, as in R2). ### Stage T1 — `tts/text.rs` + `tts/http.rs` (pure, tested) `[feat]`

- Blocked by: R5 (H6 for the live test). Files (new): `src-tauri/src/tts/mod.rs`, `tts/text.rs` (`normalize(&str) -> String`: URLs → "link", strip `* # \` > \_ ~ |`, collapse whitespace; `split_sentences(&str) -> Vec<String>`per D19),`tts/http.rs`(reqwest client, 10 s connect / 60 s read timeouts;`POST {base}/v1/audio/speech` `{model:"kokoro", input, voice, speed, response_format:"pcm", stream:false}`→`Vec<i16>`LE;`GET {base}/v1/audio/voices`; errors as `String`with a`Unreachable`prefix the overlay can detect).`lib.rs` `pub mod tts;`.
- Tests: unit tests for `normalize`/`split_sentences` (URLs, markdown, "e.g.", quotes/brackets, long paragraph fallback split, non-ASCII boundaries); `#[ignore]` integration test against 127.0.0.1:8880 asserting ≥ 1000 samples and `bm_lewis` in voices.
- Verify: per-stage list; `cargo test -- --ignored tts::` with H6 running.
- Implementation Notes:
  - **Decisions:** `mod tts;` is private (nothing outside the crate needs it). `normalize` runs line markers (`>` nesting, `#` headings only when followed by a space, `- `/`* `/`+ ` bullets) → `[text](url)`/`![alt](url)` → URLs (`http://`, `https://`, `www.`, case-insensitive, only at a word start; trailing `.,;:!?'` stay with the sentence; stops at whitespace or `<>"()[]`) → inline `*`, `` ` ``, `~` dropped, `|` → space, `_` dropped unless between two alphanumerics (`snake_case` survives) → whitespace collapse. Inline `#` and `>` are kept ("Issue #42", "x > 3"). `split_sentences` merges a fragment into the previous one when either is < 20 chars, so a short first fragment absorbs the next; lengths are counted in chars. `http::client()` uses `expect` on `Client::builder().build()` — it only fails where `reqwest::Client::new()` would panic too. Error strings: `Cannot reach TTS server at {base}`, `TTS server at {base} timed out`, `Invalid TTS server URL: {base}`, `TTS server returned {status}`, `TTS server returned malformed audio (N bytes)`, `TTS server returned an unexpected voice list: …`, else `TTS request failed: {e}`; the raw reqwest error and any non-2xx body are logged at `debug`.
  - **Deviations:** connect timeout 2 s (not 10 s) — the backend is local, so a dead server should surface fast; total request timeout 60 s. No `Unreachable` prefix: the messages are user-facing as-is; T2/T3 match on `Cannot reach` if they need to tell connection failures apart. Added a non-ignored `unreachable_server_reports_base_url` test (127.0.0.1:9, closed on macOS) that also proves `tauri::async_runtime::block_on` works in a plain `#[test]` without a running app — no dev-dep needed.
  - **Live test (H6):** `cargo test -- --ignored synthesize_against_local_server --nocapture` → `bm_lewis` listed; 77,693 samples (3.24 s of audio at 24 kHz) for a 50-char sentence; wall time 1.37 s on the first (cold) run, then 0.36–0.53 s.
  - **Learnings:** 24 new tests (66 pass / 1 ignored). `cargo build` warnings 58 → 83; the 25 new ones are all `tts::*` dead code until T2 wires `SpeakAction`. Clippy wants `as_chunks::<2>()` over `chunks_exact(2)` and `is_none_or` over `map_or(true, …)` on this toolchain (rustc 1.99).

### Stage T2 — SpeakAction, pipeline, selection capture, binding, CLI/signals, tray `[feat]`

Files: `actions.rs` (`SpeakAction` + `TtsState` + synth/playback threads per D10; overlay/tray calls direct; feedback: none), `clipboard.rs::capture_selection(app) -> Result<Option<String>, String>` (D12; macOS pasteboard snapshot module adapted from `paste_tx/macos.rs`), `input.rs` (`resolve_command_keycode(ch: u8)` + `send_copy(enigo, hold_ms)` mirroring `send_paste_ctrl_v`), `audio_out::open_output` used by the playback thread, `settings.rs` (D4 keys, `speak` binding D5, prune stale stored bindings; remove `cancel`/`test`), `shortcut/mod.rs` `change_tts_*` commands in `change_overlay_position_setting`'s shape (no register/unregister inside the handler), `handler.rs` (speak: press only), `cli.rs` (`--speak-selection`, `--stop`; drop `--toggle-transcription`, `--cancel`), `lib.rs` single-instance matcher → `run_on_main_thread` fire-and-forget; commands `stop_speaking`, `fetch_tts_voices` (async, spawns only); `signal_handle.rs` (SIGUSR2 → speak, SIGUSR1 → stop on macOS); `tray.rs` (`TrayIconState::{Idle, Speaking}`; menu `speakSelection` / `stop` (enabled while speaking) / `settings` / `quit`; tray keys seeded in 26 locales); `utils.rs` (`cancel_current_operation` → `stop_speaking`); `settingsStore.ts` entries for every new key.
- Verify: per-stage list; manual in `tauri dev` (dev permission is charged to the terminal — acceptable here): Safari selection → ⌥⎋ speaks, ⌥⎋ stops immediately; hold ⌥⎋ for 2 s → exactly one start; double-press within 300 ms → stop, no second session; tray "Speak selection" works; clipboard contents (text and an image) restored after capture; `osascript -e 'tell application "Safari" to activate'; sleep 0.5; ./src-tauri/target/debug/mouthy --speak-selection` and `--stop` drive the running instance; server down → log shows Unreachable, app stays up.
- Implementation Notes:
  - **Decisions:** `actions.rs` holds the whole loop. `speak_selection(app, source)` (`actions.rs:133`) is the single entry for the hotkey (`SpeakAction::start`), tray, `--speak-selection` and SIGUSR2; `stop_speaking` (`:174`) serves the tray Stop item, `--stop`, macOS SIGUSR1, the overlay's × button and the `stop_speaking` command. **Toggle rule:** press while Speaking → stop (`:139`); press while Capturing → ignored (`:144`); an explicit stop during Capturing cancels it and the captured text is dropped (`:192`). Ignoring rather than stopping during capture debounces duplicate deliveries of one press. With the Tauri backend the capture blocks the main thread, so a second press queues behind it and lands in Speaking (stop) anyway. **Per-session stop token:** each session owns an `Arc<AtomicBool>` and a generation (`:152`); the capture tail and the playback guard act only if their generation is still current (`:192`, `:313`). **Main thread, fire-and-forget:** the Capturing session is inserted under the `TtsState` lock, the lock is dropped, then `run_on_main_thread` runs `capture_and_speak` (`:164`); nothing ever waits for it. **UI ordering:** overlay/tray changes are numbered under the lock and applied on the main thread, which drops stale ones (`post_ui` `:83`, `apply_ui` `:93`). **Synth thread** (`:267`): the only `block_on` (`:282`), one request per sentence, stop checked before each, the first `Err` ends it. **Playback thread** (`:328`) owns the output device and the rodio `Player` (`:351`), takes the next chunk only while `player.len() < 2` (`:361`), re-checks stop between receive and append, waits on `recv_timeout(20 ms)`. `SessionGuard` (`:302`) runs on any exit (finish, error, device failure, panic): it sets the stop flag (ending the synth thread) and, if still current, clears the session and shows idle or the error notice. Selection capture (`clipboard.rs:57`) asserts the main thread (`:175`), snapshots every pasteboard item and type, sends ⌘C (layout-aware `input::command_key(b'c')`, 30 ms hold), polls `changeCount` every 10 ms for up to 200 ms, keeps polling until text is readable, and restores only if `changeCount` hasn't moved since the copy (`:210`). `overlay::show_overlay_notice` (`overlay.rs:596`) emits `show-overlay-notice` `{kind, message}` and auto-hides after 2 s (nothing-selected) or 6 s (error) only if no later show happened (generation snapshot at show time, `:506`). Tray: `Idle | Speaking`; menu = version / Speak Selection / Stop (enabled only while speaking) / Settings / Quit. Settings `tts_base_url`, `tts_voice`, `tts_speed` (clamped 0.5–2.0), `tts_volume` (clamped 0–1) with `change_tts_*` commands and `settingUpdaters`; `fetch_tts_voices` is async (awaits `list_voices`). Binding `speak` = `option+escape` (macOS) / `alt+escape`; the General page shows it instead of Cancel (also on Linux now). Removed: `CancelAction`, `TestAction`, `cancel_operation`, `utils::cancel_current_operation`, `signal_handle::send_external_input`, every `id == "cancel"` skip and the "(which is dynamic)" comment, overlay `recording/transcribing/processing` states (+ `show_*_overlay` fns and `overlay.transcribing/processing` keys), tray `cancel` key. Locale keys added to all 26 (English seeded): `tray.speakSelection`, `tray.stop`, `overlay.{speaking,nothingSelected,error}`, `settings.general.shortcut.bindings.speak.*` (replaces `.cancel`). `signal_handle` is now `cfg(unix)` as a module.
  - **Deviations:** (1) `audio_out::open_output` uses rodio's `open_sink_or_fallback()` instead of `open_stream()`. rodio asks for a fixed ~50 ms buffer (2048 frames at 44.1 kHz), and this machine's default output (Shokz Bluetooth, buffer range 14–1024) rejects it with "Error opening the stream with the OS", so nothing could play. A scratch probe confirmed it: fixed buffer fails, default buffer and the fallback succeed. The fallback only runs when the first open fails. (2) The design's "UI changes under the lock" first draft deadlocked at runtime: `set_tray_state` off the main thread calls `window.theme()`, which waits on the main thread, while the main thread waited on `TtsState` (`sample` stack). Fixed by the sequenced main-thread `post_ui` above, then re-verified. (3) Deleted the dead overlay `show_recording/transcribing/processing_overlay` here rather than in T3, because the frontend states they drove went away in this commit.
  - **Edge cases:** empty, whitespace-only or non-text copy → "nothing selected", clipboard untouched when `changeCount` didn't move (verified). An app that bumps `changeCount` before writing text → polling continues to 200 ms. A write after our read → no restore (newer write wins). A write after the 200 ms timeout → the selection stays on the clipboard (accepted, C-S5). Unreachable server → error notice "Cannot reach TTS server at …", tray idle, app up (verified). Hung server → stop still immediate (verified); the synth thread sits in its request until the 60 s timeout, then exits on the failed send. Stop then a fast restart → the old synth thread may still have one request in flight, so at most two concurrent requests. Back-to-back identical signals merge into one (OS behaviour, observed). No Accessibility → capture fails with "Enigo state not initialized" as an error notice (verified). Old stores → `cancel` pruned, `speak` added (observed in the export run). Non-macOS capture compares `read_text` to the saved text, so selecting text identical to the clipboard reads as nothing selected there; Windows/Linux not compiled (only `aarch64-apple-darwin` is installed).
  - **Verification (runtime spike, debug binary from `src-tauri`; temporary env-gated patch, reverted, checksums match):** this shell's process tree is **not** Accessibility-trusted (`AXIsProcessTrusted` = false), so: handy-keys could not register (`HotkeyManager` refuses without Accessibility); Tauri backend (store switched to `tauri`) registered `option+escape` (`is_registered` = true). The real ⌘C path fails cleanly (Enigo uninitialised); **real ⌘C capture, the ⌥⎋ keypress, the 2 s hold test and the tray click were not verified.** With text injected in place of capture: 3-sentence CLI speak played to completion (first chunk queued within the trigger second, ended 11 s later, so the device consumed audio in real time); `--stop`, second `--speak-selection` (toggle), SIGUSR2 → speak and SIGUSR1 → stop each ended playback within the same log second; stop with nothing playing is harmless; server on a dead port → error notice. With a simulated app copy against the real `NSPasteboard`: change detected (233→234), 40 chars read, both original items restored with every type (plain, RTF, `org.nspasteboard.ConcealedType`, custom), one extra `changeCount` bump. No-op copy → nothing-selected, `changeCount` unchanged. Concurrency: SIGUSR2 then SIGUSR1 50 ms later (mid-capture) ×5 → each capture discarded, no deadlock; SIGUSR2 ×2 mid-capture → second ignored. **Kokoro hung during verification** (log time is UTC; local is BST): last successful synthesis 17:15:30 (run 3, SIGUSR2 session, stopped 17:15:33); the next request at 17:17:20 (simulated-copy run) never completed; from then on it accepts TCP but never replies (0% CPU). Earlier runs between those times sent no request (dead-port and capture-error runs). It was not restarted, so the final end-to-end playback re-run on the committed code was **not run (server hung)**. The post-fix UI path was re-verified with the hung server (speak → overlay/tray Speaking, stop → Idle).
  - **Learnings:** the per-stage `cargo build` now shows 31 `dead_code` warnings, all R6: `input.rs` 4, `clipboard.rs` 8, `paste_tx/` 19 (83 before; `tts/`, `tray.rs`, `audio_out.rs`, `overlay.rs` and 19 of `input.rs` are wired). Tests 66 → 72 (+3 `poll`, +1 default-binding↔`ACTION_MAP`, +1 PCM conversion, +1 default bindings valid on both backends, +1 tray icon; −1 recording/transcribing menu). The debug binary needs the Vite dev server for its webviews; backend paths run without it. `~/Library/Logs/com.azizi.mouthy/mouthy.log` has debug-level lines with second granularity. Off-main-thread Tauri window getters (`theme()`, sizes) block on the main thread, so never call tray/overlay code from a thread holding a lock the main thread may take.
  - **For T1b/T3:** T1b's "no speakable text" skip belongs in `actions.rs` `synthesize` (the `failed` break). Already done here from the T3 wording list: `overlay.transcribing`/`overlay.processing` removed, `bindings.cancel` replaced by `bindings.speak`.
  - **Grep verification:** zero hits in `src`/`src-tauri/src` for `CancelAction`, `TestAction`, `cancel_operation`, `cancelOperation`, `cancel_current_operation`, `send_external_input`, `toggle_transcription`, `toggle-transcription`, `id == "cancel"`, `TrayIconState::Recording`, `Transcribing`, `show_recording_overlay`, `command_v_key`, `overlay.transcribing`, `tray.cancel`, `MOUTHY_SPIKE`. `block_on` appears only in `actions.rs:282` and `tts/http.rs` tests.

Tests 99 → 86 pass (+1 ignored): −7 `paste_tx`, −5 paste/auto-submit/external-script in `clipboard.rs` (5 more were Linux-only), −1 auto-submit default; `salvage_drops_only_wrong_typed_fields` now poisons `tts_speed` instead of `paste_delay_ms`. Old symbols (`paste_tx`, `send_return_key`, `write_text_to_clipboard`, `PasteMethod`, `ClipboardHandling`, `AutoSubmitKey`, `TypingTool`, `changePasteMethodSetting` etc., `clipboard_manager`, `is_wayland`) have zero hits in `src/`, `src-tauri/src`, `src-tauri/build.rs`.

- Implementation Notes:

Advanced keeps App (tray icon, experimental toggle) and the Experimental group (keyboard implementation, H8); theme and language stay on About. The voice dropdown (`ui/Dropdown`) appears only when `fetchTtsVoices` succeeds, refetches when the stored URL changes, ignores stale responses, and writes the same `tts_voice`. `settingsStore.updateSetting` re-reads settings after any `tts_*` write (`backendNormalizedSettings`), so trimmed/clamped values show (T2b DEFER). Overlay: `SpeakingOverlay.tsx`/`.css` from scratch; a local sequence drops a `show-overlay` whose async settings read finishes after a later show or hide (T2b DEFER race); listeners are now unlistened on unmount (the old effect leaked them); the fade actually runs (the old code unmounted on hide). Error view = `Speech failed: <message>` clamped to two lines + Open Settings (`showMainWindowCommand`) + × (`stopSpeaking`). Window label renamed `recording_overlay` → `overlay` with `capabilities/default.json` in the same commit; `create_recording_overlay`/`hide_recording_overlay`/`RecordingOverlayPanel` → `create_overlay`/`hide_overlay`/`OverlayPanel`; title `Mouthy Overlay` (window and `index.html`). Accent: `--*-color-logo-primary` `#4f46e5` light / `#818cf8` dark, `logo-stroke` `#312e81`/`#e0e7ff`, and `--color-background-ui` (Handy's deeper pink, used by toggles, primary buttons and slider fills) `#4f46e5` too, so no pink remains. `handleAccessibilityComplete` is a `useCallback([updateSetting])`. About acknowledges Handy, Kokoro and rodio (`ACKNOWLEDGMENTS` list).
  - **Deviations:** (1) The overlay window is sized per view (`OverlayView::size`: 256×50 for speaking/notice, 360×60 for error) instead of one size: the error card needs the width, and a larger always-on window's transparent area would swallow clicks. `OVERLAY_SIZE` remembers the last size so `update_overlay_position` keeps a visible card centred. (2) Wording: keys whose meaning changed now hold the new English value in all 25 locales (`onboarding.permissions.description`, `…accessibility.description`, `accessibility.permissionsDescription`, `settings.general.shortcut.description`, `settings.sound.outputDevice.description`, `settings.advanced.overlay.style.description` (the "Live" text), `…overlay.position.description`, `settings.debug.onboardingPreview.description`, fr `overlay.position.title` ("recording window")); `keyboardDiagnostic.running` only swaps "Option+Space" → "Option+Escape" in place, keeping translations. `settings.advanced.groups.*` had no leftovers (R6 removed them). The dev store came back byte-identical (sha `983b9ad4…`).

(6) CHANGELOG regrouped into Added/Changed/Removed/Fixed, describing the result against Handy rather than each stage; fixes to features new in this release folded into Added. Binary sizes are in its intro line.
